Core rules it brought are in: **Save Actions** (01-roll, "### Save Actions"), traits **Save** and **Shot** (08-traits), glossary entries. Added **Bow Mastery** (Archer Feature 1: 3 Mastery with one Shot weapon; flavor written by Claude, to review). Twin Shot: "with your weapon's Weapon Technique, if you have unlocked it". Escalation passives use "While your Escalation is 2 or more,". The builder writes a Save on the Vs. line as it is ("Instinct Save") and [HT: ...] traits as "HT: Entering, Turn". Open: Opportunity Attack (Talent) also makes "a Weapon Technique (1A)": same wording? Was: (new class, being revised with Lucas)
  - **Save Actions** (Save trait): no roll by the user; the target rolls the named Save against 11 + the Modifier of the Action's other trait (Weapon or Power); tiers are the target's (Tier 1 worst); a [Save, Weapon] Action adds the Weapon Modifier ([Mod]) on Tier 1 and 2; the Effects row happens on Tier 1 and 2; no Critical; [Signature, Save] = 11 + Signature Modifier, an Attribute Save Action = 11 + (Attribute ÷ 2 rounded up + 1); not a Strike; Vulnerable = banes on the Save, Cover = boons, counted from the center of the area's Point of Origin. The card only says "Instinct Save". Effect Timing (General Effects on Tier 2 or 3) needs the Save exception.
  - ~~Repeating damage~~: covered by the book's [HT] trait (Entering / Turn / Movement, turn-cycle limit) in core-rules-updated.html; Covering Fire is [HT: Entering], Rain of Arrows [HT: Entering, Turn].
  - **Bonuses added to another Action** (Effect Timing: "follow that Action's result, only on its Tier 2 or 3"): buffs also apply on Tier 1, even when the Action deals 0 damage on that tier (a missed shot with Killer's Focus still deals its 1d6). Features that add damage to other Actions will name [Strike], so they never apply to Save Actions (Killer's Focus done).
  - **Damage reduction** ("you take less damage, reduced by X"): once per Action; the creature taking the damage chooses which instance is reduced (Hunter's Focus, Hallowed Stance).
  - **Shot** (new trait, Chapter 8): needs a ranged weapon you don't throw (bow, crossbow), uses its Range; a Weapon Action gets it when used with such a weapon. Every Archer Action with Ranged also has Shot. Foundry: add the trait and the weapon check.
  - **3 Strikes per turn** counts each turn, not each round (Reaction Strikes on other turns have their own 3).

## Round 12 (applied to the book and to Foundry, v0.0.117 to v0.0.126)
- **Power is dice only:** Power Actions add no Attribute on any Tier (Chapter 8, and the Attribute Damage box in Chapter 4 is closed with it). Tier 1 damage on Power cards is intended: Power is reliable and flat, Weapons swingy and growing with the Attribute.
- **Signature** adds the Signature Attribute on **every** Tier, Tier 1 included, to the Action's first damage instance (the "nova" Actions).
- **Defenses:** Armor DC = 11 + Armor value (0 unarmored, 1 light, 2 medium, 3 heavy; medium = 13, the default; heavy rare); Ward DC = 11 + Ward value (charms, holy symbols, rings, species, features, temporary effects); Vs. Attribute unchanged (13 + half).
- **Armor** (new section, Chapter 6) with the table and **Armor Training** (Light, Medium or Heavy, each including the lighter ones; everyone is trained in Unarmored). Paladin Heavy, Archer Light. Untrained: all your Rolls are Doomed and Actions that roll against your Armor or Ward are Favored. Open box: armor properties (Resist by subtype, Bulky, Noisy, Warded), shields (Block), an unarmored Talent tree.
- **Stacking, one rule** (Chapter 2): bonuses from different effects don't add up; for each value use the highest bonus and the highest penalty. What adds up: "increase your value by N", boons and banes, AP losses. Equipment is not a bonus (it is the value). Chapter 4 (Empowered/Enfeebled) and Chapter 9 (conditions) now point to it.
- **Paladin:** Smite 2 / 3 AP Holy 2d4 / 2d6 / 2d8; Vow of Enmity Tier 2 "1 boon, and you increase your Empowered value by 1 for that Strike"; Judgment 2d6 / 2d8; Rebuke 1d4+1 / 1d6+1 / 1d8+1 (2R: 1d6+1 Pushed 1 / 1d8+1 Pushed 2 / 1d10+1 Pushed 3); Hand of Mercy also removes an effect with 1 round left.
- **Balance notes from the review (level 1, Modifier 2, DC 13):** a full turn (Smite 2 AP + two Weapon Attacks) ≈ 22.5, on the 22 benchmark; Smite 2 AP now equals Smite 1 AP + a Weapon Attack with a bane (12.3); 1 boon ≈ +0.5 damage on a Weapon Attack, +0.7 on a Smite; per +1 DC a Weapon Attack loses ~9%, Power cards that hit on Tier 1 only 2-3.5% (Ward matters most for Power effects on Tiers 2-3 and Power cards with nothing on Tier 1). Hallowed Stance kept as is.

## Round 11 (applied to the book and to Foundry, v0.0.117 to v0.0.126)
- **Allies** (Rules Language): you are never your own ally; a rule that includes you says "you or an ally".
- **Specific beats general** (Rules Language): a card or feature that differs from a general rule wins for that case (example: Fists use Armor).
- **Values that change between Versions** (Reading an Action): one value per Version, in cost order, separated by slashes; target counts in words ("One / one / two creatures"), mechanical values in numerals ("5 / 10 squares", "Aura 2 / 3 / 3").
- **Defenses** (Chapter 2, new subsection under Versus): DC = 13 + a Defense. **Armor** vs Weapon Actions (and Fists, by their own rule), **Ward** vs Power Actions; Signature uses the Defense of the Modifier it rolls, Split the one of its mode; a **Vs.** entry replaces them (13 + half the Attribute, rounded up); an Attribute Action with no Vs. entry has DC 13. Armor and Ward are 0 unless armor, a feature or a stat block gives more. "+1 Defense" raises all of them; "+1 Armor", "+1 Ward", "+1 Defense vs Body" only that one. Open box: armor values (about 1 to 3, with drawbacks) and where Ward comes from.
- **Turn** Frequency: once per turn, on your turn and on each other creature's turn. Card color teal (#0F6E6E), between At-will green and Round blue; can be changed in `FREQ_COL`.
- **Aura** (Chapter 3, Areas): an area that follows its origin, from every square it occupies (Emanation footprint); Point of Origin always that creature; the origin decides whether it is affected; you gain the effect when you enter and lose it when you leave.
- **Heal** (Chapter 4, Rally Points): to heal N is to remove N damage; the creature chooses which damage.
- **Tags** (Chapter 8): creatures have Tags (Profane for Profane Judgment); listed in stat blocks; no rule by themselves. Open box: the list of Tags.
- **Paladin rewritten**: level 1 is Divine Power, Profane Judgment, 4 Paladin Picks (1 Encounter, 1 Daily, 2 from any list) and 4 Escalation Picks (1 Passive, 3 Actives); Divine Connection is gone. Locked Actions: Smite, Lay On Hands. Free Picks: Aegis, Vow of Enmity, Shield of Faith, Rebuke, Sacred Weapon, Champion's Challenge, Mercy's Reward. Encounter Picks: Word of Radiance, Divine Retribution (2 / 3R; the 3R adds an Appeal Save in an Emanation 2), Aura of Protection. Daily Picks: Miraculous Recovery, Consecrated Ground, Divine Intervention. Escalation: Divine Vigil (now an Aura 3), Avenger's Oath, **Hallowed Stance** (was Consecrated Stance), Call to Arms, Divine Surge, Hand of Mercy, Judgment, Sanctified Ground.
- **Conversions in those cards**: Vow of Enmity, Champion's Challenge, Word of Radiance and Judgment roll Power with no Vs. entry (so they face Ward); Rebuke is Divine • Power, Turn, "Trigger: An enemy within Reach damages an ally"; "attacks" became "[Strike] and [Power] Actions"; "heal" kept as the new defined word; Divine Intervention: "instead of going Down, the creature's total damage becomes 1 less than their Health Pool"; Consecrated became **Mercy's Reward** (only the Lay On Hands trigger); Sacred Weapon raises Empowered from any source; Divine Retribution's 3R Save has its pool ([M]).
- **Builder**: the Frequency moved from the meta line to the title bar ("Category | Frequency | cost"); the Turn color; Reaction costs with several Versions ("1 / 2R"); a Split card's cost badge.
- **After review**: distances in squares are written "5 sq." (and the rule says so); trait words are highlighted only where they name the trait (inside [brackets], or before Action, trait or property), not in "Weapon Modifier", "Divine Power" or a "Weapon" distance.
- **Choices I made**: Aegis, Aura of Protection and Shield of Faith keep "+N Defense" (all Defenses); Mercy's Reward is used up by the next Smite; Consecrated Ground's bane covers [Strike] and [Power] Actions.

## Round 10 (applied to the book and to Foundry, v0.0.117 to v0.0.126)
- **Power** replaces Magic everywhere: Power trait, Power Modifier, Power rolls, Power Actions (Lesser Blessing and Rimebrand are Divine • Power; Strained and the open box on Attribute damage say Power).
- **Distinct Choices** (Chapter 5): a class's choices between two Attributes can't repeat one: Attribute increases among themselves, Weapon and Power Modifiers between themselves; the two kinds don't limit each other. The old "every choice is independent" text is gone.
- **Modifiers from the higher Attribute:** each class lists two Attributes for Weapon and two for Power; each Modifier uses the higher (÷ 2 + 1, rounded up), never the sum. If one Attribute is the higher for both, one Modifier uses its other Attribute: the player chooses which and can change it at level-up. The Attribute behind a Modifier (Weapon / Power / Signature Attribute) is what a Tier 2 or 3 adds to the damage.
- **Signature Modifier** = the higher of Weapon and Power (choose if equal). **Signature** trait: rolls it, never needs a weapon.
- **Roll kinds** (Chapter 1, Rolls): Check, Save, Weapon roll, Power roll, Signature roll, Attribute Action, each roll exactly one; "[Attribute] Rolls" covers all of them (for Weapon, Power and Signature rolls, the Attribute behind the Modifier). Table of examples.
- **Classes:** Paladin Weapon Body or Appeal, Power Mind or Appeal. Archer Weapon Instinct or Body, Power Mind or Appeal.
- **0 AP and 0R** replace Free Actions: 0 AP only on your turn (Release, Call to Arms, Divine Surge); 0R a Reaction that costs no AP. Linked Actions cost no AP as part of their Action. Stopping Leaning costs nothing.
- **Weapon Range** (Chapter 3): Reach is the body (touch, interact, Reposition, Lay On Hands, Trip...). Every weapon has a Range, melee too (Greatsword 1); a single number has no far or maximum range; Reach above 1 adds to every number of a melee weapon's Range; Distance **Weapon** = the Range of the weapon used (Weapon Attack, Smite, Reaping Sweep, Lock & Loose, Cut Through, Pummel, Leg Sweep). New properties **Long** (1 bane against adjacent creatures, doesn't threaten them) and **Thrown** (a Range in hand and a thrown Range; thrown = Ranged).
- **Melee and Ranged** come from the weapon and how it is used (thrown = Ranged); an Action with the trait needs a weapon used that way.
- **Threatening** uses the normal range of the [Melee] Action (not the far range; not adjacent enemies with Long). The flanking example and diagram caption say Range 2.
- **Escalation** (Chapter 2): per PC, 0 at the start of an encounter, +1 at the start of every round after the first, up to 3; spent on Escalation Actions (cost shown as "1 Escalation" on top of AP); kept between rounds; no gain while Unconscious (kept, not spendable); back to 0 when the encounter ends, and every effect it created ends; NPCs have none. Passives work while Escalation is at least N. Each class: 1 Passive and 3 Actives chosen at creation (step 8 of Building a Character).
- **Paladin Escalation** (Chapter 5): Passives Divine Vigil, Avenger's Oath, Consecrated Stance ("since your last turn", Power Attribute); Actives Call to Arms (0 AP), Divine Surge (0 AP: "the next time you deal damage with Smite, you deal an extra [M] 1d8 Ho"), Hand of Mercy, Judgment (now Divine • Power, tiers reversed: No effect / 1d6 / 1d8, Hindered EoT on Tiers 2 and 3), Sanctified Ground.
- **Split** trait (Chapter 8): Weapon or Power mode chosen at each use; the Action is a Weapon Action or a Power Action for every rule; only the traits and the damage differ. **Divine Retribution** (Paladin Feature 1, 2R, Encounter) is the first one; the card shows a column per mode.
- **Stacking:** the Divine Surge example.
- **Builder:** the Power, Signature, Split and Long chips; "**Escalation:** N" shows "N Escalation" first in the card's line; a table with mode columns ("Weapon", "Power") draws a Split card; passive Escalation abilities look like passive Talents; the sample card's Distance is Weapon.
- **Choices I made that you may want to change:** Divine Retribution's Distance is "Weapon (Weapon mode) or Reach (Power mode)" and its Trigger says "within the Distance of this Action"; Judgment has no Strike trait (as you wrote it); Sanctified Ground has no Target line (the text says enemies in it); Divine Surge lasts until that Smite or the end of the encounter.

## Applied on October 9 (log)
- **Round 1:** Hidden and Unsensed give Favored (Senses table, examples, glossary); lights reach a circle measured to the square's center (the edge counts); the section is called **Recovery**; Exhausted lowers the Health Pool but not Rally Points; Prone and Hindered linked (replaced in round 2); Several Targets says the targets are chosen first (an Area is placed before the roll); stronger grey for the Divine and Focus chips; the Damage Subtypes table with each type's color and icon; the sample card in Chapter 1 in the parchment style.
- **Round 2:** Prone rewritten (Melee Actions against you gain 1 boon, Ranged Actions suffer 1 bane, you can't Evade, the only [Move] Actions are Crawl and Stand); new Basic Action **Crawl**; Evade (2 AP) rounded down; Release has Self; new rule **Engaged** and section **Ranged Combat** (Chapter 3); Unconscious: "You can't be Engaged"; the sample card is a dark parchment on the dark page; the two-letter damage codes (Ho, Sl...) are colored like their types.
- **Round 3:** Ranged Combat: shooting at a creature Engaged with a creature **other than yourself** is 1 bane; Unconscious: "While Unconscious, every creature is Unsensed to you" and the hourly healing only "while Unconscious and not Dying nor Shattered".
- **Round 4:** Engaged no longer depends on perception (an enemy next to you that you can't see still Engages you); only conditions that say "You can't be Engaged" (Unconscious) prevent it. Decided with the example of Nari hidden from Zack next to him and Drak shooting at Zack from far away.
- **Round 5 (build):** the book builder had no color for the Daily Frequency (the legend says purple, but a Daily card came out grey). Daily is purple (#5B3FA6, as in Foundry) now. The book itself did not change, since no card is Daily yet.
- **Round 6 (tools):** added the index, the conventions note (`rules-src/README.md`), the term search (`--where`) and the build checks (`rules_tools.py`). One link fixed: the glossary entry "Setup" pointed to the last heading called "Actions" (the Archer's, in Chapter 5) and now goes to the first (Chapter 2). Repeated titles now always resolve to the first.
- **Open from the checks (for your call):** "Actions" (Chapter 2 and each class), "Melee" and "Ranged" (weapons and traits) are titles used in more than one chapter, so a `[[Melee]]` link would go to the weapons section; the glossary entry "Category" is not mentioned by any chapter; 34 rule headings have no glossary entry (the list is printed by every build).

## Round 7 (applied to the book)
- **Hearing:** "You perceive creatures that make noise within 10 squares as Hidden, even through walls." (Senses; glossary entry Hearing.)
- **Charge:** the card and the "Straight lines" bullet say the Charge ends in the last **empty** square where the line is legal, even with movement left. An unwilling creature's space is Difficult Terrain, so it stops a Charge; an ally does not, but you can't end in its space.
- **Flanking** (new section in Chapter 3): a creature threatens every enemy within its Reach for as long as it can make a [Melee] Action that deals damage (for example a Melee weapon in hand; Fists only with Brawler); use the longest Reach of those Actions; an Unconscious creature threatens no one. Flankers gain 1 boon on [Melee] Actions per other flanker, up to 3. A creature threatened by two or more enemies is Flanked and does not flank; count every threatening enemy first, so if everyone is flanking, no one is. Unconscious now also says "you threaten no one".
- **Fists (corrected):** the Fists Basic Action does **not** have the Melee trait, by intent: to flank with Fists you need the Brawler Talent (it makes your Fists a Melee Weapon). I had added the Melee trait to Fists and rewritten the Melee trait entry because of it; both are reverted, and the Flanking text says so.
- **Diagrams:** see above. The Pushed diagram was redrawn on the shared drawing code and highlights exactly the same squares and steps.

## Round 8 (applied to the book)
- **Flanking, SWADE-style.** A creature that flanks an enemy gains 1 boon on its [Melee] Actions against it for each other creature that flanks it (up to 3); each ally of that enemy that threatens the flanker cancels 1 of those boons; never fewer than 0. The "Flanked" status and its sentence ("if everyone is flanking, no one is") are gone, and so is the glossary entry Flanked; the entry Flanking says it. Reason: a Flanked creature's exclusion cancelled every attacker next to the defender's ally, so three skeletons around a Paladin with an ally beside two of them got nothing.
- **Examples:** two, three and more flankers (the limit of 3); a Reach 2 weapon; three goblins on Kai with Nari next to two of them (2, 1, 1); two lines of fighters (only the fighters at the very ends gain 1, against the second fighter of the other line).
- **Diagram:** six panels (the last two are new); the "Flanked" ring is gone.

## Round 9 (applied to the book and to Foundry v0.0.105; the list below is what was decided)
- **Dark fights.** Under the Senses table: "You gain no Favored or boon from a creature's poor perception of you if you perceive that creature as Hidden." Two creatures that are Hidden to each other are Doomed only (the old sentence "Hidden to each other: Favored and Doomed, normal" goes). Asymmetric cases are unchanged. Dim light is unchanged (mutual Obscured: 1 bane against 1 boon cancels). Foundry: the Favored from the target's perception needs the attacker not to perceive the target as Hidden.
- **Movement words.** Only Move Actions are "Move" (an Action with the Move trait: Advance, Charge, Crawl, Stand); never a bare capital "Move" as a verb. "You haven't moved" is your own movement (Advance, Charge, Crawl, Evade; not Stand; not Forced Movement). "You haven't left your square" is any change of your square, Forced Movement included (a Large creature: any change of the squares it occupies, "space"). A small box in Advance and Evade with this table, and matching glossary entries: Advance/Charge/Crawl = Move Action, moved, left your square; Evade = not a Move Action, moved, left your square; Stand = Move Action, not moved, not left; Forced Movement (Pushed, Reposition...) = not a Move Action, not moved, left your square. Features say which "since": "since your last turn" is since the end of your last turn, "this turn" is since the start of your turn. Foundry: store the square at the start and the end of each turn; a flag set only by your own movement.
- **Reactions: Interrupt and Trigger.** The card's line says the kind: "Interrupt: ..." resolves before the Action that triggers it; "Trigger: ..." resolves after it. Both cost 1R, once per triggering Action, and several creatures can react. Only Interrupts can disrupt. Foundry: store the kind; no automation (used by hand).
- **Opportunity Attack** becomes an Interrupt: "A creature within 3 squares leaves a square during a Move Action or uses the Stand Action." Once per triggering Action (a multi-square Advance is one trigger).
- **Disrupting part of an Action.** Disrupting an Action that is part of a larger one stops the larger one only if that part is necessary for it. What already happened stays, the AP stays spent, and a disrupted movement stops in the square the creature was in. A Charge disrupted before its 3 necessary squares is cancelled whole (no Weapon Attack); disrupted after them, it ends there and the attack happens if there is a valid target.
- **"Moves closer to you."** Interrupts: per step (a square it enters is nearer to you, 1-2-1-2 count, than the one it left); Triggers: the whole movement may be compared. Lateral steps that don't change the count don't count. Forced Movement doesn't trigger Reactions; Evade doesn't either.
- **Movement cap.** When you move with an Action you can't move more than your current Speed (when the movement starts), per movement and not per turn; Forced Movement is not capped; abilities that go beyond it say so or change Speed (Battering Charge: "Speed counts as 2 higher").
- **Stacking.** The same effect never stacks unless it says so; a repeated one keeps the higher value and the longer duration; "increase your ... value by 1" stacks; boons and banes are the exception. Applies to all named conditions.
- **Terms.** Creatures get **Tags** (TBD; not "traits", which belong to Actions). New trait **Fear** (no rule of its own; other features reference it). Cut Through's Setup: "Evade 2" (not "Evade up to 2").
- **Empowered / Enfeebled: decided (A).** Empowered N adds N to every damage die; Enfeebled N subtracts N from every die, each die at least 1; no hard caps yet. (Before: being redesigned.) Decided so far: both have a value N; the same effect doesn't stack; Empowered and Enfeebled net (Empowered minus Enfeebled) and the result applies once to each damage instance; flat numbers are untouched. The ladder (1d4 ... 2d12, then +1, +2...) with Enfeebled's floor at 1d4 is not final; options are being compared. Talents: Battering Charge (Empowered 1), Bloodlust (Empowered 1), Reload Shot (Empowered 2: the only one that gets stronger).
- **Waiting for you:** the Reactions text; the Talents (Patient Hunter and Consecrated Stance are TBD examples of "haven't moved" and "haven't left your square").

- **Still waiting for you:** the Reactions text; the Talents (Patient Hunter and Consecrated Stance are TBD examples of "haven't moved" and "haven't left your square"); Tags have no content yet.
